# Multi-Level Work Breakdown Structure (WBS)

> **Companion design document**. This document accompanies
> [RFC-039 Compiler Architecture Refactoring](../../rfc/accepted/039-compiler-architecture.md),
> expanding the RFC’s phase sequence into **three levels of tasks that can be committed and verified
> independently**.
>
> The phase sequence and acceptance gates are governed solely by the main body of RFC-039. This
> document is responsible only for decomposing the work into executable granularity and registering
> conflicts that have not yet been adjudicated.

## Positioning and Scope

| Level       | Count       | Meaning                                                                                          |
| ----------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| **Level 1** | 11 (P0–P10) | Phases, corresponding one-to-one with RFC-039’s phase sequence                                   |
| **Level 2** | 73          | Task groups: work groups decomposed by phase, each able to form and archive a team independently |
| **Level 3** | 268         | Work units that can be committed and verified independently                                      |

**Reserved numbering gaps (not reused)**: P4 has no `4.7`/`4.8`, and P8 has no `8.1`; after the
LALRPOP route was withdrawn, `8.4` / `8.5` / `8.6` were also left unused (2026-11-25). All three
have had no corresponding entries since the WBS was established—the full history of this document
has zero hits for the three headings `**4.7` / `**4.8` / `**8.1`
(`git log -S'**4.7' -- docs/src/dev/architecture/09-execution-wbs.md` can verify this), and there
are no repository-wide references. `8.1` corresponds precisely to phase 1 of `05` (test revival and
completion). That phase has been decomposed into P1 (`1.1`–`1.3`) and `8.8`; P8’s numbering
therefore aligns with its phase numbers (phase 0 → `8.0`, phase 2 → `8.2`, and so on).

**Covered by this document**: task decomposition, dependency relationships, parallelization groups,
and binding of acceptance criteria.

**Not covered by this document**: the design rationale for tasks (see `02`–`08`), or the
justification for phase ordering (see RFC-039).

**Registration convention (effective 2026-10-12)**: every newly registered Level 2/Level 3 row
**must contain two fields**—“**Owning phase**” (the P to which it truly belongs, which may differ
from the section in which it appears) and “**Blocks mainline**.” `Registered, awaiting scheduling`
is no longer the default state; an unmarked task is considered **not schedulable**. Numbers must be
**unique throughout the document** (cross-track tasks use their track number, such as `029g.x`, and
must not occupy `N.x`).

**Closure tracking (effective 2026-11-24)**: a checked row contains only four items—the task ID,
status, **implementation commit short hash** (7 characters), and issue number, if any—in one line:
`- [x] **3.24.1 Remaining pattern-matching work** ✅ 4d608df3 (issue #405)`. **Do not write the fix,
the criterion chain, gate values, file line numbers, test names, or review conclusions**: those
belong in the commit message (`git show <short-hash>`). This table is a dashboard, not a process
chronicle. **Do not write adjudication labels or restate conclusions**: “user adjudication A/B/C,”
“Plan A,” “Option A,” and “the final implementation is…” must not appear in this table—the dashboard
answers only “is it complete?” Consult the commit messages and RFC-039’s decision registry for
adjudications and results.

**Short hashes are authoritative after push**: a closure row records the short hash on `origin/dev`
after the push has completed. The order is **push the implementation commit first, then write the
closure row** (see [commit convention](../commit-convention.md), “Commit hashes are obtained after
push”). The closure commit itself does not carry a hash—self-reference is impossible.

**The count column contains only numbers**: the Level 1/2/3 counts under `Positioning and Scope`
contain only current values; increment/decrement history (`+1 = …`) does not belong in the table.

## Current State: Gaps Found During Decomposition

While consolidating the “Implementation Points” of eight documents into this table, **five hard gaps
in the documentation system itself** were found. All have now been resolved:

| #   | Gap                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Resolution                                                                                                                                    |
| --- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| G1  | **P5 has no implementation steps whatsoever.** RFC defines P5 (checker splitting) and its acceptance criterion (C1 zero-diff), but none of the seven documents contains a section describing how to perform the split. The directory tree in `01-routing.md` contains one `annotations.rs` entry, the only mention in the entire batch.                                                      | P5’s steps are supplied by this document (see below)                                                                                          |
| G2  | **The three ownerships of the `include!` refactor have zero implementations.** RFC lists it under P4; `02` assigns the reference to itself; `06` S7 also lists it. However, the change list in `02` (6 additions + 20 modifications) contains **no `semantic_tokens.rs`**.                                                                                                                   | The implementation steps are assigned to §P5 5.1 in this document (prerequisite: P4 complete); references in RFC and `06` now point here.     |
| G3  | **Three coexisting lists of CI scripts.** `RFC` lists 4 `scripts/ci/*.py` scripts; `01` lists 4 scripts + 1 `cargo test`; `07` lists 4 different checks; `04` also listed `check-synth-boundary.py` (that script was cancelled by the user on 2026-11-24). After deduplication, there are **at least 9 new scripts and no unified list**; `check-obligations.py` is owned by both P4 and P9. | The unified list appears in §P9 of this document (10 active scripts, each owned by one phase); all other documents refer to that table.       |
| G4  | **Conflict over the fate of `precedence_inline.rs`.** `06` counts it among the “1,005 lines requiring revival,” while B6 in the same document says it “must be deleted together with the `Precedence` enum”; `05` advocates deletion.                                                                                                                                                        | **Revive in P1, retain in P8** (decision registry C1; Pratt is no longer removed).                                                            |
| G5  | **Conflict in the wording of the L2 objective:** RFC and the index in this directory previously said “grammar-driven syntax.”                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | It has converged to **a declarative precedence table + an unambiguous language surface** ([05](05-frontend-paradigm.md) §Syntax); see P8 8.3. |

## Target Design: Phase Sequence

```
P0 仓库维护机制与代码放置规程 08 ← 新增，先立规矩
P1 复活孤儿测试并修缺陷 06 §S1
P2 建立等价性判据基线 07 全部
P3 修正确性漏洞（最小方案） 02 §S3-漏洞半部
P4 阶段契约与统一 Driver 02
P5 checker 文件内拆分 02 + 本文补齐
P6 类型表示单一化 03
P7 中间表示 SSA 化 04 } 可并行
P8 前端范式变更 05 }
P9 防反弹门禁（含统一脚本清单） 01 §防反弹 + 08 §可机检规则
P10 其余清理与状态修正 06 §S2/S3/S4/S5/S6
```

**Reason for adding P0**: the three prohibitions (no fabrication / no endless filling / no patching
when a refactor is required) constrain every action in P1–P10. The rules must be established before
work begins; otherwise, reviving tests in P1 would already involve adding `mod` declarations to
`tests/mod.rs`, which is precisely the source of the G4 conflict.

### P3 Fix Correctness Defects (Minimal Plan)

Source: [02](02-stage-contract.md) §Implementation Points S3, defect-fix half + **2026-10-07
Silent-Discard-Point Audit (3.4)**

> **Semantic-correction routing (D59, adjudicated 2026-10-11)**: if this track or a later phase
> discovers a defect for which “the old behavior did not count as success”—**A silent failure / B
> silent mismatch / C undefined behavior and uncontrolled resources** (crash, stack overflow,
> silently incorrect result)—it **may be fixed along the refactoring path**, but all four
> requirements are mandatory: ① red before green (the red criterion must already exist and genuinely
> fail); ② do not change the externally observable behavior of programs that “previously compiled
> successfully and ran correctly” (they must not become failures, and their existing diagnostic
> codes/text/exit codes must not be changed or removed); ③ register in the same commit (this row
> identifies the error category + that phase’s revision note + the PR required block’s “Error
> category” line); ④ regenerate the baseline in the same commit + manually review the diff. **D
> user-visible semantics / E external contract table / F RFC-level design changes must still be
> adjudicated first.** See [07 §Routing Criteria for Semantic Corrections](07-equivalence-oracle.md)
> for the expanded criteria and A–F allowlist; **the “default super-refactor + retroactive
> registration” approach is not adopted** (rationale in RFC-039 D59: with no red criterion, fixes
> cannot be distinguished from regressions, and this conflicts with “C5′ does not exist” and “no
> relaxation whatsoever”).

> another carrier of the same “named argument” criterion—stdlib native exports), so **they are
> merged into one team**. The team scope is relaxed from “one Level 2 task” to “a batch of related
> small items”; the batch composition, rationale, and deviations must be registered when that team
> closes.

| 3.22 Decomposition semantics of function-type annotations (issue [#501](https://github.com/ChenXu233/YaoXiang/issues/501); source = follow-up from batch-review F4 in 4.10 round 2): the **parenthesized function type** in the return position has the wrong hierarchy when decomposed—“a function that returns a function type” has **no valid annotation syntax**; the annotation itself parses correctly (the parser was ruled out by measurement). **Owning phase: P3; Blocks mainline: No**                                                | 3.22.1 See below                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `src/frontend/core/typecheck/inference/statements.rs`                                                                                                                                                           | —                                                                                | **Final criterion**: see 3.22.1                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
|                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | 3.22.1 Unify annotation decomposition for a “function returning a function type”: the value-parameter group of `() -> ((x: Int) -> Int)` = `()`, and the return = `fn(int64)->int64`; the inner `Fn` **must not be absorbed into the parameter group**; align the expected shape on the lambda-body path as well (currently there are two sets). The criterion covers four forms: lambda body / function-name RHS / no inner parentheses / scalar-return control (✅ 7880ee17, f032582b; issues [#501](https://github.com/ChenXu233/YaoXiang/issues/501), [#514](https://github.com/ChenXu233/YaoXiang/issues/514), and [#515](https://github.com/ChenXu233/YaoXiang/issues/515))                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | src/frontend/core/parser/ast.rs、src/frontend/core/parser/statements/declarations.rs、src/frontend/core/typecheck/inference/statements.rs、src/frontend/core/typecheck/tests/paren_return_decomposition.rs      | —                                                                                | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

D59 **Category C** (silently gives the wrong error code): red before green—the red state = `'\x1'` reports `InvalidToken/"Empty character literal"` (measured; see the commit message for task 8.8); the green state = it still reports `InvalidEscape` (E0012), with the position pointing to `\x`; do not change programs that “previously succeeded” (`'\x1'` already failed); do not change the code registry (InvalidEscape still maps to E0012)                   |

**Acceptance**: defect criteria turn green, and **deliberately removing the fix must turn them red
again**. **Risk**: adding E4018 to multi-file paths is a breaking change.

**3.4 acceptance**: 3.4.1 / 3.4.2 must have red criteria first (changing code first is forbidden);
3.4.3 is manual review; 3.4.4–3.4.8 use C2/C3 and the 9.2 gate in P9 as the criteria. **Risk**: the
new diagnostic in 3.4.1 is a behavioral change and requires synchronizing the corpus differential
baseline and CHANGELOG.

> **Stop-the-bleeding path**: P3 need not wait for all of P2. The only hard prerequisites for
> 3.1/3.2 are **2.3.2 (multi-file corpus layer) + 2.4.1/2.4.3 (two red criteria)**;
> 2.1/2.2/2.3.1/2.3.3 (IR verifier, snapshots, single-file differential, performance baseline) may
> proceed in parallel with P3. Stopping correctness defects (`Sorted(3)` passing silently) must not
> be blocked by snapshot-infrastructure work. 3.3 (panic-to-diagnostic) has no prerequisite and may
> land at any time.

### External Track RFC-029g: Remove `pub` and Automatic Binding (External Track / #399 — Ordering Constraint D56)

Source: [RFC-029g](../../rfc/accepted/029g-remove-pub-and-auto-bind.md) (accepted 2026-10-02,
implementation not started), [#399](https://github.com/ChenXu233/YaoXiang/issues/399). **This track
is not a refactoring design item itself; it is a specification-compliance deletion forced by an
ordering constraint**, and is distinct from P10 S5 “reduce the visibility of `pub` items” (Rust’s
`pub(crate)` narrowing).

**Why it must wholly precede P4**: 029g’s deletion surface overlaps the rewrite surface of all four
refactoring phases. Reversing the order means repeating the same deletions and baseline updates on
the new grammar / new checker layout / new SSA IR.

| 029g deletion surface                                               | File                                                                                                               | Rewritten by which phase                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KwPub` and syntax branches                                         | `lexer/{tokens,state,mod}.rs`; `parser/parser_state.rs`, `statements/{declarations,imports,functions,bindings}.rs` | **P8** (8.2 lexical convergence / 8.3 Pratt precedence convergence / 8.7 split `parse_assign_after_target`)                                                                                                                                                           |
| AST `is_pub` ×2 (`Assign` / `TypeDefinition`)                       | `parser/ast.rs`                                                                                                    | **P6 6.5 AST dead variants** + P8 + D1 directory renaming (`parser/ast.rs` → top-level `src/ast/`)                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `auto_bind_to_type` and the `pub` branch of `collect_exports`       | `typecheck/checker.rs`, `environment.rs`                                                                           | **P5** (`include!`→true `mod` and checker splitting are **pure moves**; the `pub` branches are moved unchanged into the new files)                                                                                                                                    |
| Dead-code exemptions `exempt_pub` / `is_exported` and role dispatch | `typecheck/passes/dead_code.rs`, `module/orchestrator.rs` (inside `check_project`)                                 | **P4 4.2.2** (slim down `check_project`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `semantic_tokens`’s `Public` modifier                               | `typecheck/checker/semantic_tokens.rs`                                                                             | **P4 4.2.6** + P5 + 4.6 (D55)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ir_gen`’s `is_pub`-ignoring binding                                | `middle/core/ir_gen.rs`                                                                                            | **P7** (`ir_gen.rs` SSA conversion)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| 31 `pub` occurrences under `src/std/`                               | `std/list.yx` / `json.yx` / `option.yx` / `result.yx`                                                              | No phase directly modifies these, but **P6’s hard acceptance requires no differences under `src/std/` (`git diff --stat src/std/` is empty; direct-drive tests under `tests/` are exempt as described in P6’s hard-acceptance section)**—they can only land before P6 |

**Additional benefit**: 029g removes the dead `env.exports` table (filled by production code and
consumed nowhere) and the `is_exported`/`is_visible` accessors ⇒ P4’s 4.3 obligation ledger has one
fewer “produced but never consumed” field.

- [x] **029g.1 Lexer and parser branches**
- [x] **029g.2 AST `is_pub` ×2**
- [x] **029g.3 Checker side**
- [x] **029g.4 Dead-code exemption consolidation**
- [x] **029g.5 Peripheral feedback**
- [x] **029g.6 std and diagnostic text**
- [x] **029g.7 Language-reference convergence**

| Level 2                    | Level 3                                                                                                                      | File                                                                                                                 | Acceptance                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| -------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 029g.1 Lexer and parser    | 029g.1.1 Delete the `KwPub` variant and its `keyword_from_str` entry                                                         | `lexer/tokens.rs`, `state.rs`, `mod.rs`                                                                              | Keyword table 18→17 (`Kw*` 16→15, including the count comment in `tokens.rs`)                                                                                                                         |
|                            | 029g.1.2 Delete `pub` detection at declaration positions and `pre_detected_pub`                                              | `parser/statements/declarations.rs`, `functions.rs`, `bindings.rs`                                                   | `pub f = ...` reports a parse error (**red criterion first**)                                                                                                                                         |
|                            | 029g.1.3 Delete `pub` skipping for entries inside `use` braces and the statement-leading `pub`                               | `parser/statements/imports.rs`, `parser_state.rs`                                                                    | `pub use m` reports a parse error (**red criterion first**)                                                                                                                                           |
| 029g.2 AST                 | 029g.2.1 Delete `is_pub` from `Assign`/`TypeDefinition` + synchronize construction sites                                     | `parser/ast.rs` and all construction sites                                                                           | No `pub` remnants in the compiler (`grep -rn is_pub src/` is empty)                                                                                                                                   |
| 029g.3 Checker             | 029g.3.1 Delete `auto_bind_to_type` (`pub` was its only reason for existing)                                                 | `checker.rs`, `environment.rs`, call site `checker.rs`                                                               | The method form has only explicit combinations (RFC-004); std and corpus have zero dependencies                                                                                                       |
|                            | 029g.3.2 Remove the `pub` branch from `collect_exports` + delete the dead `env.exports` table and `is_exported`/`is_visible` | `checker.rs`, `environment.rs`                                                                                       | The sole export authority = module registry `ModuleInfo.exports`; one fewer field in the 9.2 gate                                                                                                     |
| 029g.4 Dead-code exemption | 029g.4.1 Delete `exempt_pub` / `is_exported` and the exemption branch                                                        | `dead_code.rs`                                                                                                       | Unreferenced top-level bindings in Lib/Script roles report W1001 (**red criterion first**)                                                                                                            |
|                            | 029g.4.2 Synchronize role dispatch in `orchestrator`                                                                         | `orchestrator.rs`                                                                                                    | Single-file / multi-file diagnostic sets are normalized (C2); W codes do not mix into consumers                                                                                                       |
| 029g.5 Peripheral feedback | 029g.5.1 Formatter’s `pub` prefix                                                                                            | `formatter/handlers/stmt.rs`                                                                                         | `format` is idempotent (existing criterion)                                                                                                                                                           |
|                            | 029g.5.2 `semantic_tokens`’s `Public` modifier                                                                               | `typecheck/checker/semantic_tokens.rs`                                                                               | Semantic highlighting no longer emits Public                                                                                                                                                          |
| 029g.6 std and text        | 029g.6.1 Delete 31 `pub` prefixes under `src/std`                                                                            | `std/{list,json,option,result}.yx`                                                                                   | Corpus and std self-checks are green; **must be completed before P6**                                                                                                                                 |
|                            | 029g.6.2 Diagnostic text                                                                                                     | 4 W1xxx help texts                                                                                                   | No longer claims that “pub is an external interface”; aligned with the Bin-role behavior in `dead_code.rs`                                                                                            |
| 029g.7 Documentation       | 029g.7.1 Language reference and guides                                                                                       | `reference/language-spec/{modules,syntax,language-overview}.md`, `warning-code/warning-codes.md`, `guide/modules.md` | The specification no longer contains statements contradicting the implementation that “pub can still be written”; the “pub never triggers W1001” statement in `warning-codes.md` is corrected as well |

**Acceptance (behavioral changes, red criterion first)**:

1. **Red before green**: each of the two behavioral changes must have a red criterion before code is
   changed—① `pub` changes from “silently accepted” to a parse error; ② unreferenced top-level
   bindings in Lib/Script roles newly report W1001 (previously `pub` items were absolutely exempt).
2. **The test count must not decrease**: when deleting the `exempt_pub` case from
   `typecheck/passes/tests/dead_code.rs` and the `is_pub` fixture, they must be replaced by
   equivalent new cases.
3. **Baseline in the same commit**: diagnostic-set changes (the new parse error + new W1001) ⇒
   update the corpus differential baseline (`tests/baselines/corpus-parity.jsonl`) and CHANGELOG in
   the same commit; the IR snapshot is expected to have zero diff, and any non-zero diff requires an
   explanation.
4. **Independent revert unit**: the entire batch occupies one commit group and must not be mixed
   with other phases (DoD 6).
5. `cargo test` / `clippy -D warnings` / `python scripts/rfc/check_tracking.py` all pass.

**Ordering**: it overlaps 3.4 in files (`checker.rs`, `environment.rs`) ⇒ the two must be serial and
cannot run in parallel; **it must wholly precede P4** (RFC-039 D56). The 029g track does not depend
on 3.4; the implementer chooses which to start based on the work in hand.

> **Commit comparison (2026-10-08)**: the incorporation of this section (P3.5 chapter +
> main-chain/constraint row) and RFC-039’s D56 were folded into `97071c81b` while workspace cleanup
> was sweeping the parallel stream—the commit message records “3.4 supplementary registration +
> D57/D58 adjudication filing” and does not mention this track. History is not rewritten; following
> the established convention (the `d5b18390` precedent, see the P2 landing note), it is registered
> in place as evidence for later bisection.

|                                            | 4.4.4 The SMT shared-solver observation ledger `ConsumerStats`/`CONSUMER_STATS`: every production-path acquisition is written, and the production side has zero reads (the only read site is `proof/smt/tests/shared.rs`). **Decision ① retain — implement 2026-10-24**—codify “the sole consumer = tests” as a doc contract (preserving D58’s observable-criterion carrier); decision ② “delete it” was not selected (it would require rewriting 4.4.2’s acceptance criterion at the same time, with the trade-off rationale in the 4.4 closure “sixteen”). **Final state**: the three contract sentences are written into the module header and relevant item comments of `src/frontend/core/typecheck/proof/smt/backend.rs`—① the write surface = one atomic increment for every acquisition through the shared interface; ② the read surface is **only** `proof/smt/tests/shared.rs` (zero production calls); ③ `SolverConsumer::ALL` is a **test-only constant** and production code must not depend on it; **zero behavioral changes** (only comments changed, and 4.4.2’s acceptance figures are unchanged; issue [#475](https://github.com/ChenXu233/YaoXiang/issues/475)) |                                            | 4.9.9 Cross-unit single-definition method calls lose the formal-name table when using **named arguments** (registered 2026-11-25, discovered during the 7c closure round): a cross-unit (registry-injected) method called with named arguments reports `E1014 Function '…' has no parameter named '…' (available: )`—the name table is empty; the same method’s positional-argument call works, and named-argument calls within one file work. Minimal reproduction: `lib.yx` defines `Point.scale_by: (self: &Point, k: Float) -> Float`; in `main.yx`, `p.scale_by(k = 2.0)` ⇒ E1014, while `p.scale_by(2.0)` ⇒ 6.0. **D59 classification: Category C** (old behavior was not success: named arguments were silently dropped, followed by runtime failure or a silently wrong value). **Scope (final form after two narrowing rounds on 2026-11-26—only three additions; everything else is deletion and registration)**: ① `AstToIrGenerator::method_param_info` (`src/middle/lower/query.rs`)—use the defining unit’s qualified name `{module}.{Type}.{method}[#N]` to obtain declaration-time formal names from the sole cross-unit carrier `ModuleInfo::method_overloads` (candidate ↔ mixed IR name ↔ formal name are three same-order vectors, using the same source as typecheck injection); the call site (`src/middle/lower/expr/call.rs`) uses `method_dispatch_name`’s qualified name to look up the name table instead of two bare-name segments. These two locations fix only **bare-name imports** (`local == target`) for cross-unit methods: candidate injection retains the pre-4.9.9 narrowing rule (`local != target` is skipped), so named arguments for alias imports are outside the fix scope (see “Known Gaps”). ② `ModuleRegistry::all_method_bindings` (`src/frontend/module/registry.rs`) merges in **ascending module-key order**—**Category C (independent of the original bug): eliminate “same input, multiple results” caused by HashMap iteration order**, not a fix of the original bug; criterion = same-file unit test `test_all_method_bindings_merge_is_deterministic_across_hash_seeds` (32 registry instances with alternating insertion order; only one single binding is permitted for the same method key; removing the ascending merge produces two results). **Criterion (red before green)**: 2 driver-level cases (`src/driver/tests/named_args_cross_unit_method.rs`) (bare import, single candidate 6.0 / two candidates selected by name, 6.0 and 30, all with value assertions; the file header records the pathology gate); unit test `src/middle/lower/tests/query.rs` (`method_param_info` empty-name-table guard ⇒ None, positive control returns a non-empty table unchanged); unit test `src/frontend/module/registry.rs` (the deterministic criterion above). The project fixture `tests/yaoxiang-multifile/named-args-cross-unit-method` (the `alias-overload` fixture is deleted along with retirement of the alias surface); the corpus differential baseline `tests/baselines/corpus-parity.jsonl` now has 404 lines: **relative to the direct parent commit `ac810e97`, 405→404** (the intermediate state included `alias-overload`); **relative to the pre-4.9.9 baseline `08ccacc5`, 403→404** (+1 base fixture `named-args-cross-unit-method`; `alias-overload` never entered final history). The line-count change = an unavoidable consequence of fixtures entering and leaving (after `corpus_probe` regeneration, it is byte-for-byte identical to the committed baseline), **not lost coverage**. **Known Gaps (each named explicitly; old behavior = silent ignoring or silent dropping; D60② should have reported but did not, Category A)**: closure-value binding / block-body function declaration / named nested function declaration / named arguments for built-in container constructors other than `len` / whole-module import / RFC-004 binding arm. **Two items use the P0 measurement-corrected convention**: ① **static-function forms** (both intra-unit and cross-unit, definition-style and binding-style) are **entirely unavailable today**—intra-unit `check` reports E3006; cross-unit definition-style `run` reports E6007; cross-unit binding-style `run` reports E6006; positional and named arguments produce the same code, so this is unrelated to named-argument dropping; ② **RFC-004 cross-unit positional arguments also fail** (`check` passes, `run` reports E6006), because the dispatch name is absent from the merged function table, also unrelated to named arguments. **Fixed (4.9.10-S2b, incidental Category A fix: old behavior E6007/E1014 was not success, so no previously successful program becomes a failure)**: ① **alias-import named arguments** (`use a.{Temp as X}` + `t.bump(k = 2.0)`); ② **namespace arms** (named arguments after `lib.Temp(..)`); ③ **inferred-receiver named arguments** (the receiver type is inferred and has no written annotation). These previously produced runtime E6007 with silent argument dropping and check-time E1014 `(available: )` false rejection, respectively, and now all bind by declaration-time formal name (value assertion 6.0; criterion `src/driver/tests/named_args_import_shapes.rs`, with the red state recorded in that file’s header pathology gate). Two identity-surface residuals remain, one of which is closed: an intra-unit same-named type + alias import (two same-named definition-side identities coexist, and the import surface cannot register the local declaration) **has been closed with 4.9.10-S2a’s import-surface consolidation** (the project fixture `same-name-type-local-and-alias-import` is fully green); the remaining one is: function-body-level / cross-scope imports are merged globally by unit (shared import table)—this form itself does not work (existing defect: `check` passes, `run` reports E6006), was not introduced by this batch, and remains unfixed. **Deletions in this round (relative to the six rounds before 4.9.9)**: candidate-provider priority, candidate signature comparison, owner/compatibility inference, the shared provider table in `frontend::module::import_face`, the IR-side “no name table ⇒ E1014” fallback, and the **E5006 ambiguity prohibition at the import-name resolution point**—the latter rejects mixed/alias-only forms that actually ran on the parent commit (Category D); the 2026-11-26 adjudication withdrew it; this round introduces no new ambiguity determination. **Visible changes**: ① cross-unit method named arguments (**bare-name import**, including two-candidate overloads) change from E1014 with an empty table to binding by name (measured 6.0 / 30); ② all other forms return to pre-4.9.9 behavior (named arguments silently ignored or dropped, see “Known Gaps”); ③ the single method binding for the same input no longer varies with HashMap iteration order (Category C). **Status**: completed on 2026-11-26 (including the narrowing round and review pass). | 4.9.7         | The criteria are in `src/driver/tests/named_args_cross_unit_method.rs` and `src/driver/tests/named_args_import_shapes.rs` (the latter added with 4.9.10-S2b), and `src/frontend/module/registry.rs`; the root fix is in 4.9.10                                                                                                                                                      |
|                                            | 4.9.10 Cross-unit method identity is undefined (registered 2026-11-25, structural problem confirmed during 4.9.9 implementation): the method candidate key `{Type}.{method}` does not include module qualification, so when same-named types exist across modules, three consumers—candidate injection (`driver::arms::visible_method_overloads`), typecheck’s formal-name/candidate matching, and IR dispatch (`method_dispatch_name`)—each guess the provider by name, and changing any one moves the “silently wrong value” to another. **Root solution**: upgrade type identity to **module-qualified identity** (`{module}.{Type}`), and change the method candidate key to `{module}.{Type}.{method}`; one resolver produces it and three consumers only read it. This requires changing the shape of `overload_resolutions` keys and mixed IR names; D59 **Category D** (requires adjudication). **Disposition in this round (after the two narrowing rounds following 4.9.9)**: 4.9.9 **introduces no new rejection**—the previous rounds’ Category D rejection list (all named arguments rejected with E1014 / positional arguments in conflict forms always rejected with E1107 / E5006 ambiguity prohibition at the import-name resolution point) is deleted and all of it is void; mixed / alias-only / conflict-form **import forms and positional arguments** that ran on the parent commit are restored to working order. **Residual surface after this round closes**: ① function-body-level / cross-scope imports are merged globally by unit (shared import table), and this form itself does not work (existing defect: `check` passes, `run` reports E6006); ② named arguments are still silently ignored or dropped in some forms (D60② Category A): **static-function forms** (intra-unit `check` E3006; cross-unit definition-style `run` E6007; cross-unit binding-style `run` E6006—positional and named arguments produce the same code and are unrelated to named arguments), **RFC-004 cross-unit** (positional arguments also report E6006), **closure-value binding**, **block-body function declarations**, **built-in container non-`len`** (forms such as `size`: named arguments are ignored, `length` is 0, and runtime reports E6005; the `len` form works normally). **Closed with this round**: ① method candidate keys / method keys changed to module-qualified `{module}.{Type}.{method}` (L0’s `DefPath` dual representatives + S2a’s table keys and single-point resolution; all three consumers named at registration—`visible_method_overloads`, formal-name reverse lookup `method_param_info`, and `method_dispatch_name`—were removed); ② an intra-unit same-named type + alias import (two same-named definition-side identities coexist); ③ alias-import / namespace-arm / inferred-receiver named arguments (three forms turn green, see the corresponding section in 4.9.9). **L3 type-identity residuals (migration surface, unfixed; red at L0 and not a regression in this round)**: ① type names themselves are still not module-qualified—the static type name in `MonoType` is the **definition-side bare name**, so same-named types across units still cannot be distinguished at the type layer and must rely on the import surface + binding identity; ② `VarInfo::type_identity` covers only **local-binding** registration sites (written annotation / constructor write path), while parameters, pattern bindings, closure parameters, and `for` variables are not registered—the receiver at these locations can still be resolved only by static type name (`set_type_identity` has only one call site in the repository). **Status**: L0 / S1 / S2a / S2b implemented; L3 (type-representation-layer identity, D64) completed L3-0 / L3-1a / the type-conversion funnel / S1 (method-key entry point) / S2 (driver project helper) / S3 (index derived from `env.types`), with S4 onward proceeding by DAG. ### P5 Splitting checker.rs into Files — Filled In by This Document (Original Gap G1)

Source: RFC definition + the directory tree in `01-routing.md` + hard constraint (the `pub(crate)`
path of `collect_used_in_type` must not change)

- [x] **5.1 `include!` → true `mod`** (2 Level 3 tasks)
- [x] **5.2 Extract `refinement`** (2 Level 3 tasks)
- [x] **5.3 Extract `annotations`** (2 Level 3 tasks)

| Level 2                     | Level 3                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Prerequisite | Acceptance                                                                                                                                     |
| --------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 5.1 `include!` → true `mod` | 5.1.1 Change the `include!` at the end of `checker.rs` to a true `mod` declaration (line anchors are not hard-coded; measured at the end of the file at the time)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | P4 complete  | C1 zero-diff; `semantic_tokens.rs` gains module identity                                                                                       |
|                             | 5.1.2 Add `use super::*` or individual imports (the `include!` era implicitly inherited the scope)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | 5.1.1        | `cargo build` is green                                                                                                                         |
| 5.2 Extract `refinement`    | 5.2.1 Move out the refinement blocks (three non-contiguous ranges from the original `checker.rs`; now one file, `checker/refinement.rs`, containing `ReturnRefinement` / `RefinedWalkCtx`), leaving the public surface and 6 cross-boundary shared items in place                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | 5.1.1        | C1 zero-diff                                                                                                                                   |
|                             | 5.2.2 Consolidate the refinement entry point as “`&self` + return diagnostics,” **without leaving the impl**: `collect_refined_binding_checks` (now `checker/refinement.rs`, `pub(super)`) no longer uses `&mut self` to implicitly write to the error channel; the diagnostic set is returned upward, with the sole write site in the sole caller `check_module_impl` (`checker.rs`, symbol location). **Reason for remaining in the impl**: the only family field is `self.env` (technically it could leave the impl), but moving both the entry point and the walk to free functions would also move call sites: `resolve_type_annotation` has 8 production sites (6 annotation paths + `refined_walk_stmt` + `collect_refined_var_names_in_stmt`), and `collect_param_refinements` has 2 production sites + 1 test site, and would create a reverse dependency from the retained surface (signature collection) to refinement—the opposite of the coupling this step is intended to eliminate | 5.2.1        | —                                                                                                                                              |
| 5.3 Extract `annotations`   | 5.3.1 Move out 10 members of the annotation-validation family (now one file, `checker/annotations.rs`; `check_annotation_type_names` is the `pub(super)` entry point, the other 9 are private to the family, and `is_predicate_head` is a free function within the family)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | 5.2.2        | C1 zero-diff                                                                                                                                   |
|                             | 5.3.2 **Keep `collect_used_in_type` (`pub(crate)`) as `pub(crate)` under the `checker` module path**: it is a **shared-surface** function (consumed by both `collect_function_signature` and `inference/statements.rs:16`), and measurement in 5.3.1 confirmed that it **was not migrated** ⇒ the “zero call-site changes” criterion **was already satisfied**, so **do not add** a `pub(crate) use annotations::*;` re-export. **Constraint retained**: if it is ever moved with the family (into annotations or another later batch), `checker.rs` must add `pub(crate) use annotations::*;` so that the import remains unchanged                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | 5.3.1        | **“Zero call-site changes” is this step’s hard acceptance criterion: `git diff src/frontend/core/typecheck/inference/statements.rs` is empty** |

**Hard constraint**: `collect_used_in_type` is used across modules by `inference/statements.rs:16`.
**If** the function is ever moved with the annotation family, **then** `checker.rs` must add
`pub(crate) use annotations::*;` so that the import remains unchanged; measurement confirmed that
5.3 **did not migrate it**, so this batch adds no re-export (the criterion was already satisfied).

**Only split confirmed large blocks.** Do not split modules of merely 200–400 lines—this adds
navigation cost, reduces cohesion, and yields no decoupling benefit. The remaining submodules
(signatures / type_defs / imports) are to be split when their files grow back to 1,500 lines.

**6.9 Model and Invariants (First-Principles Rewrite, 2026-11-13)**

> **Model**: D62’s end state is a **unified item world**—type positions and value positions share
> the same compile-time item. One intensity declaration produces one quantifier reading
> `q ∈ {0,1,ω}`; `q` is the **sole input** to slot allocation, and the call-site argument view and
> implementation-header binding view are two projections of the same fact. Equality is a **pure
> decision** (identical after normalization on both sides; does not bind type variables); evidence
> is the **sole entry point** for q=0 slots (four sources: literals/value environment/single-value
> pinning/Γ equalities); erasure is the **runtime consequence** of q=0 (and holds for closed, open,
> and recursive terms); Σ construction sites **reuse the same evidence interface** rather than
> defining another one. **The completion criterion = the invariants hold, not that a substage name
> has been checked off.**

> **6.9 closure criterion**: I1–I5 and I7 all hold, I6 is closed according to the design-review
> conclusion for 6.9.5 (does not block P7), and I8 has evidence for every slice; residual items
> #537/#539/#540/#541/#542/#552 all have a disposition (fixed/explicitly excluded/separate case);
> #542 landed on 2026-11-19. **Do not substitute “all substages checked off” for the invariants
> holding.**

> **Partial order (not a serial chain)**: A facts → {A.C3, B.d, C.2, D design}; B kernel → {B.d,
> C.2, D implementation}; C.1 is independent and complete; F4 adjudication → A.C3; decision-point
> protocol → B.d; design review → D implementation. A.C3 and B.d may run in parallel but require
> file-surface coordination (C3 leans toward typecheck→mono, while d leans toward
> types/solver→sema); C.2 may run in parallel with B.d after 6.9.2-B/6.9.3a (it does not depend on
> defeq); D’s implementation waits for confirmation of the B design package; it depends on A (q=0
> erasure) and C.1 (dispatch), and may depend on 6.9.3’s typed exit. **The P7 gate = the A/B/C exits
> of 6.9**; D does not block.

> **A.C3’s prerequisite: F4 adjudication (A adjudicated on 2026-11-16, with measurement)**: the old
> wording “`CheckOnly` evidence does not enter requests” is obsolete—`ConstEvidence`/`CheckOnly`
> have been deleted; the current mechanism is that open-term `normalize` returns `Unproven` ⇒ no
> request is created, and the representation layer’s `GenericArg::Const(ConstExpr)` can already hold
> `k-1`. **The adjudication changes to two orthogonal decisions + one termination subdecision**: D-a
> representation (甲 open terms enter `deferred` and are normalized before enqueueing / 乙 do not
> specialize); D-b evaluation point (mono only; 丙-1 second field and 丙-2 reconstruction from IR
> both break the single representation/evaluator, 丙-3 = 甲); **F4-T termination** (naive firing
> pushes the recursion chain to `max_depth` and hits E3005; T-a instantiate the instance’s internal
> fold condition / T-b bounded firing + fall back to the original at the boundary / T-c a
> termination layer or amend the specification). **Implement first, then adjudicate; before
> measurement, do not treat “甲 is already implementable” as a conclusion.** **User (2026-11-16):
> D-a=甲-闭, D-b=丙-3, F4-T=T-b, bound=8; C3 implementation is a separate task and does not claim
> full I4.**

> **B.d’s prerequisite: decision-point protocol (not yet written)**: RFC-027 §12.1 stipulates that a
> decision point first follows unification; after unified failure, it may attempt equality, but must
> first roll back partial bindings produced by the unification attempt. The implementation must
> answer: is the entry point an outer wrapper around `solver.unify` or a new `decide` layer; may
> defeq be attempted only when both sides are ground (have no unbound variables); what are the
> snapshot/restoration boundary and cost; do unify and defeq share one budget or have separate
> budgets. **Responsibility boundary**: unify remains responsible for subtyping/Any/Never; defeq
> only decides equality and does not take over subtyping; bidirectional subtyping must not be
> treated as equality (RFC-027 §12.1). **First provide the protocol and production call graph, then
> write code**; 6.9.3c’s wiring contract already wasted two rounds because it assumed `can_assign`
> was the decision point, while `AssignmentChecker` has zero production instances.

> **E acceptance and residuals (I8, cross-cutting)**: every slice must provide ① zero
> corpus/IR-snapshot drift or an item-by-item classification; ② `testsuite run fast` + affected
> integration tests; ③ error-code-generator idempotence (if emission sites/code registry change); ④
> classification of behavioral changes under D59 and red before green. **Wiring tasks additionally
> require**: production call-graph evidence (which production entry point, how many paths, which
> paths are reachable); unit-test reachability must not be used as a substitute for user
> reachability; rollback and budget require measured figures. **Residual disposition**: #537
> (Category D, proof-function parameter refinement is silently discarded; not fixed before
> adjudication, and 6.9.4/6.9.5 must not depend on its fix), #539 (import-surface q=0; a C-phase
> boundary, and if it remains open, 6.9.4 covers only intra-module cases), #540 (deep-nesting stack
> overflow; **closed**—3.20.30 closed on 2026-11-14: orchestration-layer 64MB large-stack thread +
> parser depth gate MAX_PARSE_DEPTH=1024 + E0019, see 09’s 3.20.30 row), #541 (TailCall;
> **closed**—three coordinated changes on 2026-11-16: `call_form_is_rewritten` /
> `replace_calls_in_function` / `drop_erased_call_args` now cover `Call`/`TailCall` through the
> single-point `rewritten_call_target`; init sections and function bodies share one site-rewriting
> implementation; red→green + three mutations), #542 (isolated shared layer; **implemented
> (2026-11-19)**: DCE pass + post-mono two entry-point connections + C2c temporary workaround
> removal; red→green, 402/402, function-table attribution and deviations), #552 (imported
> multi-parameter predicate proof execution; in the 6.9.5 neighborhood, separate case). **Ownership
> awaiting adjudication**: `rfc027_refined_transparency`’s four open-constraint tripwires
> (`Eq(N, n)`) do not belong to 6.9.4’s constant-equality bridge; decide whether to leave them in
> 6.9.4’s outOfScope or reassign them to 6.9.1/E4018’s proof-function surface.

> **Do not claim / boundaries**: this task covers only record Σ (B: witness field), does not touch
> Π/`DepFn`, and does not claim a complete Π/Σ implementation; 6.9.4 covers only constant equality
> and does not fulfill the open-constraint `Eq(N,n)` tripwire; `f(y)(y)`’s second value position is
> outside this task; witness erasure ≠ Runtime check erasure; if #537 is not fixed, 6.9.4/6.9.5 only
> cover forms that do not depend on proof-function parameter refinement; if #539 is not fixed, 6.9.4
> covers only intra-module cases; #540 is closed (3.20.30); #542 is implemented (2026-11-19);
> 2.1.4’s inner-isolation red state was cleared on 2026-11-20 (its criterion was rewritten by slot
> identity in the 2.1.4 closure) ⇒ all prerequisites for promoting `verify` to a gate are cleared.

**Hard acceptance** (**revised on 2026-10-27, following the 2026-10-26 adjudication ②**):
`git diff --stat tests/yaoxiang tests/baselines src/std/` must be **empty in every round of
6.1–6.7**. **Direct-drive test files** under `tests/` (tests that directly drive changed code, such
as `tests/formatter_integration.rs`) **may be modified when mechanically required**, but each
modification must record **file / line count / reason**; **tests must not be deleted and assertions
must not be weakened**. **Exception**: 6.8.2 is a **behavioral-fix** batch (new diagnostic codes +
baseline updates), does not fall under this hard acceptance criterion, and is a separate round
requiring separate adjudication.

> **Revision relationship**: the original convention (2026-10-26 adjudication ②) was written as
> `git diff --stat tests/ src/std/`, applying a zero-difference requirement to the entire `tests/`
> directory; the revised convention **changes only direct-drive test files from “zero difference” to
> “may be changed, but file / line count / reason must be registered”**, while the zero-difference
> requirement for the **corpus layer (`tests/yaoxiang`), baseline layer (`tests/baselines`), and
> standard library (`src/std/`)** remains unchanged. First application = 6.1.2: only
> `tests/formatter_integration.rs` changed, with **3 lines** (2 `assert_format_eq` occurrences of
> `i64`→`Int64` + 1 comment line), reason = the expected type names in assertions must be
> synchronized with the changed spelling; no test was deleted and no assertion was weakened. See “P6
> 6.1 Name Canonicalization Closure.”

### P7 Convert the Intermediate Representation to SSA

Source: [04](04-ssa.md) §Implementation Points batches a–d, criterion C4, **strictly serial within
each batch**.

- [x] **7.0 Pure-move split** (6 Level 3 tasks)

> **7.0 design basis and boundaries**: the target layout comes from the directory tree and migration
> table in [01-routing](01-routing.md)—`middle/ir/ir_gen.rs` → `middle/lower/`,
> `sema/inference/expressions.rs` → `sema/infer/` (the call-resolution family goes under `call/`).
> This batch **only performs pure moves**, with acceptance defined as “zero semantic diff apart from
> paths” (manual file-by-file review) + C1 snapshot zero-diff. It does not conflict with
> [RFC-039](../../rfc/accepted/039-compiler-architecture.md) alternative A’s rejection of “split
> files only, without changing the paradigm”: what was rejected was using a move **in place of**
> paradigm work, whereas this batch is a **prerequisite** for paradigm work—7c.1’s `lower/synth.rs`
> depends on the `lower/` directory being in place, and 7d.1’s `generate_call_expr_ir` method-level
> split operates on smaller files after the move. The expressions side follows the adjudication
> (split only the call-resolution family; `statements.rs` remains in place); the overall
> `inference/ → infer/` rename and merging statements are part of a full migration, outside this
> batch, and require a separate design round after P7. `synth.rs` is a new boundary rather than a
> move and belongs to 7c.1. Survey baseline (2026-11-12): `ir_gen.rs` is 9,804 lines with 148
> methods, dominated by a single `impl AstToIrGenerator` block (about 9,250 lines); `expressions.rs`
> is 6,825 lines with 93 methods, dominated by a single `impl ExpressionInferrer` block (about 6,280
> lines); the largest methods are `infer_call_expr` at 1,671 lines and `generate_call_expr_ir` at
> 866 lines (the latter is the target of 7d.1).

**Risk**: the precedence table is the sole authority; a missed consumer silently changes
associativity (no error, only a different parse result). Defenses are the permanent D1–D3 assertions
and operator-property cross-validation tests; every new operator must enter both the table and the
cross-validation tests.

> **8.3–8.6 depend on D0/D2 from `08`**: if the grammar file is placed outside `parser/`, or action
> code references `sema` in reverse, `check-boundary.py` will stop it.

### P9 Anti-Rebound Gates (Unified Script List — Original Gap G3 Filled In by This Document)

Source: [01](01-routing.md) §Anti-Rebound Mechanism + [08](08-maintenance-mechanism.md)
§Machine-Checkable Rules

**This is the sole authoritative list of all CI scripts** (10 active scripts; row 9.8 is retained as
a cancellation record). Scripts are connected to CI at their “introduction phase”; those marked
report-only become hard failures in P9 (changing from “report” to “fail”). Existing violations
(parallel representations, disambiguation aliases, etc.) must wait until P6/P8 to reach zero;
turning the gates into hard failures too early would keep CI permanently red and cause the gates to
be manually bypassed. When P9 turns them into hard failures, the gates should be exactly green. P2’s
2.5, P4’s 4.3.2, and P7’s 7c.1 all refer to this table and do not define separate lists.

- [x] **9.12 Prerequisites for hard failures: re-baseline criterion-C wiring points and clear stale
      inventory** (2 Level 3 tasks; Owning phase: P9; Blocks mainline: No)
  - [x] **9.12.1 Re-baseline criterion-C wiring points**
  - [x] **9.12.2 Expand criterion-C coverage and clear `build_registry`**
