## Implementation Points: Dependencies and Parallelization

### Mandatory Serial Main Chain

`P0 → P1 → P2 → P3 → P3.5 → P4 → P5 → P6 → (P7 ∥ P8) → P9 → P10`

> **P3 stop-the-bleeding path (adjudicated 2026-10-05)**: P3’s prerequisites are narrowed to
> **2.3.2 + 2.4.1 + 2.4.3** (multi-file corpus layer + two red criteria). The remainder of P2 (2.1
> IR verifier, 2.2 snapshots, 2.3.1/2.3.3 differential and performance baseline) may proceed in
> parallel with P3. Stopping correctness defects is not blocked by criterion-infrastructure work;
> however, **P4 must still wait for all of P2**—the unified Driver requires all three criterion
> layers to be in place.

| Constraint                | Rationale                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| P0 → P1                   | The three prohibitions constrain every P1 action (1.2.x adds `mod` declarations to `mod.rs`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| P1 → P2                   | Reviving tests changes the test count and corpus baseline                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| P2 (after narrowing) → P3 | Defect criteria must be **written as red first** (2.4.1/2.4.3) + the multi-file corpus layer (2.3.2) must exist so the red tests have somewhere to run                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| P3 → P4                   | Fix bugs before refactoring; reversing the order causes bugs to become fixed as “established behavior” by the stage table                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| P3.5 → P4                 | RFC-029g’s (#399) deletion surface covers P4 4.2.2 / P5 / P6 / P7 / P8’s respective rewrite targets (the list appears in this document’s §P3.5)—reversing the order means repeating the same deletions on the new grammar, checker layout, and IR (D56)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| P4 → P5                   | The `include!` refactor and checker split modify the same file                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| P5 → P6                   | Consecutive changes to the same file must be separated, otherwise regressions cannot be bisected                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| P6 → P7 / P8              | If type representations do not converge first, the new IR grows into a third representation; **the P7 gate = the A/B/C exits of 6.9** (I1–I3, I5, I7; namely 6.9.2 including C3, 6.9.3 including d, and completed 6.9.4), while D (Σ) does not block; P7 may consume the quantifier-derived view and defeq interface but must not create a parallel ledger; the 7.0 pure-move batch is a prerequisite for P7 and is not subject to this gate, but it must not consume an undecided defeq/quantifier interface |
| P7/P8 → P9                | The 9.5 snapshot baseline and 9.3 `pub(crate)` leak count reach their final form only after P7/P8                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

### Parallelizable Groups

| Group                                    | Members                                                                            | Basis                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ---------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **A (the only group authorized by RFC)** | P7 ∥ P8                                                                            | Disjoint file sets: `ir.rs`/`ir_gen.rs`/`bytecode.rs`/`translator.rs` vs `lexer/*`/`parser/*`                                                                                                                                                               |
| B                                        | 1.2.1 ∥ 1.2.2 ∥ 1.2.3 ∥ 1.2.4                                                      | Each independently adds a `mod`; revert file by file                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| C                                        | 2.1 ∥ 2.2 ∥ 2.3.1 ∥ 2.3.2 ∥ 2.4                                                    | The three criterion layers are mutually independent                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| D                                        | 4.4 (proof-layer completion) ∥ the entire P6 track                                 | `02` explicitly says S5 “should proceed independently of S1–S4”                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| E                                        | 4.2.5 ∥ 4.2.6 ∥ 4.2.8                                                              | All are mutually independent after 4.1                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| F                                        | 9.3 ∥ 9.9 ∥ 9.10 (the three scripts introduced in P0 can be developed in parallel) | The 9.5 snapshot baseline must be taken last                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| G                                        | S3 ∥ S4 ∥ S5                                                                       | Disjoint file sets (verified 2026-10-05: S3 modifies `docs/src/dev/design/check/` + `config.js`; S4 modifies `docs/src/rfc/` + the `check_tracking.py` generator; S5 modifies two `pub` occurrences under `src/frontend/core/typecheck/`; no pair overlaps) |

**Cannot run in parallel (file overlap)**: P1(1.1.x) ∥ P8(8.0.x); 1.2.1 ∥ 8.3.2; 6.0 ∥ S2 (the B6/E5
batch uses the same files); 4.3.2 ∥ 9.2 (unified under P4); **3.4 ∥ P3.5** (`checker.rs` /
`environment.rs` are the same files—the external track’s `pub`-deletion batch and the silent-channel
fixes must be serial).

**New internal document constraints**: 6.0→6.1→6.2→6.3→6.4→6.5→6.6 is strictly serial; 7.0 →
7a→7b→7c→7d is strictly serial (7.0 is the prerequisite pure-move batch for P7), and 7b’s `Phi` arm
must precede 7d.

## Key Decisions and Rationale

- **P0 is an independent phase rather than part of P9**—gates are “post-hoc checks,” whereas rules
  are “pre-hoc judgment.” P9’s scripts cannot prevent “patching when a refactor is required”; only
  D0–D4’s review checklist can.
- **P9 is the sole authoritative list of CI scripts**—the three coexisting lists behind original gap
  G3 made closure impossible. This table merges them, and 10 active scripts each have one owning
  phase (9.8 was cancelled by the user on 2026-11-24), with report-only status and hard-failure
  timing explicitly traceable.
- **P3.5 incorporates RFC-029g rather than leaving it as an “independent track to execute after
  refactoring”**—its deletion surface overlaps P4/P5/P6/P7/P8’s rewrite surface (RFC-039 D56).
  Deferring it until after refactoring means repeating the same deletions on the new grammar,
  checker layout, and IR; this is the same lesson as incorporating 7e’s `2315`/`2341` and S4’s
  RFC-status corrections into the main chain.
- **P5’s steps are supplied by this document rather than folded into P4**—P4 is already the largest
  single-point risk, and adding checker splitting would exceed the rollback granularity.
- **5.3.2 uses “zero call-site changes” as its hard acceptance criterion**—the split must be
  independently revertible.

## Known Limitations and Risks

- **1.3.x (fixing exposed defects) has no regression criterion**—it only says “the fixed defects
  must not be rolled back” and lacks a requirement to record what was fixed. **Execution
  convention**: every 1.3.x fix commit message must reference the fixed test name and defect
  description as evidence for later bisection.
- ~~**7b.1 granularity is too coarse**~~ **Fixed (2026-10-05)**: split into 7b.1a / 7b.1b as two
  exclusive commits per DoD 6.
- **8.2.1 granularity is too coarse**—about 89 renamed references; missing one **silently changes
  associativity**.
- ~~**D17 / D20 have no corresponding Level 3 tasks**~~ **Fixed (2026-10-05)**: D17 landed as 7e.1,
  D20 landed as 4.5.1/4.5.2; opcode reclamation for D32/D34 was incorporated into 7e.1, with
  deletion left to P10 S6.
- ~~**The `2315` / `2341` data-loss defects were originally intended as an “independent issue”**~~
  **Fixed (2026-10-05)**: `2341` was incorporated as 7e.3; `2315` was rechecked on 2026-11-28 and
  found to be an audit false positive (the field has no producer, and the language layer has no
  throw/try/catch), so 7e.2 was cancelled and the exception remnants moved to P10 S6.
- ~~**E2 (`lib.rs:568/582` `todo!()`) has no phase ownership**~~ **Fixed (2026-10-05)**:
  incorporated into P10 S2.
- ~~**F7 (opcode gate extractor) has ambiguous ownership**~~ **Fixed (2026-10-05)**: incorporated
  into the generation-time gate task in P10 S6.

## Conflict Registry: All Adjudicated

> **The original “Conflict Registry” C1–C8 and the open questions in each document have all
> converged into the [RFC-039 Decision Registry](../../rfc/accepted/039-compiler-architecture.md)
> (D1–D50).** This document retains no pending items. Item-by-item mapping:

| Original number | Issue                                                                          | Adjudication                                                                                                                                                                |
| --------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| C1              | `precedence_inline.rs` is revived in P1 and deleted in P8                      | **Revive it** (P1’s premise is reviving tests with real assertions); P8 no longer deletes the file (Pratt is retained)                                                      |
| C2              | “Grammar-driven” commitment vs table-driven implementation                     | **Fallback approach**: retain Pratt + one declarative precedence table + same-source cross-validation of the two `BinOp` sets (the LALRPOP route was withdrawn), see P8 8.3 |
| C3              | Conflict between C6 category definition and S1 classification                  | **S1 belongs to C6** (revival does not require an equivalence criterion, only regression tests); the C6 definition is expanded to “pure deletion or pure revival”           |
| C4              | `test_release_plan_spans_consumed` allowlist                                   | **No allowlist**; the difference must be empty; if it cannot be empty, the `ReleasePlan` contract is defective ⇒ change to `PlanId` (D20/D41)                               |
| C5              | Internal differences between the two `Aggregation` functions were not verified | **P4’s 4.2.7 has already listed it as a mandatory verification item**; implementation must confirm it line by line                                                          |
| C6              | The wasm-playground fix requires P4’s `ProgramKind`                            | **The wasm-path defect fix is incorporated into 4.2.8**; P3 handles only the three native entry points                                                                      |
| C7              | Ownership of `method_def_ordinals`                                             | **Assigned to 04** (P7 batch c), see D21                                                                                                                                    |
| C8              | Fix `upvalue_count` and the `.42` version number                               | **Fix it + raise `VERSION` from 4 to 5** (the format header already has `MAGIC` + `VERSION` fields, `codegen/bytecode.rs:14-16`), see D17                                   |
